90→ * Convert a PrintArea to CSS percentage values (relative to a square container).\n    91→ * Used by mockup-generator for server-side rendering on the 1024×1024 canvas.\n    92→ */\n    93→export function printAreaToCSS(area: PrintArea) {\n    94→  const canvasSize = 1024\n    95→  return {\n    96→    left: `${(area.x / canvasSize) * 100}%`,\n    97→    top: `${(area.y / canvasSize) * 100}%`,\n    98→    width: `${(area.w / canvasSize) * 100}%`,\n    99→    height: `${(area.h / canvasSize) * 100}%`,\n   100→  }\n   101→}\n   102→\n   103→/**\n   104→ * CSS preview zones for the frontend text overlay on real Printify product photos.\n   105→ *\n   106→ * These are DIFFERENT from PRINT_AREAS — they define where the printable zone\n   107→ * appears visually in typical Printify product photography (flat-lay, on-model, etc.).\n   108→ * Values are CSS percentages relative to the product image container.\n   109→ */\n   110→export interface PreviewZone {\n   111→  top: string\n   112→  left: string\n   113→  width: string\n   114→  height: string\n   115→}\n   116→\n   117→export const CSS_PREVIEW_ZONES: Record<string, PreviewZone> = {\n   118→  'tshirt':     { top: '28%', left: '27%', width: '46%', height: '34%' },\n   119→  'hoodie':     { top: '32%', left: '25%', width: '50%', height: '30%' },\n   120→  'mug':        { top: '22%', left: '8%',  width: '56%', getPreviewZone(category?: string | null): PreviewZone {\n   128→  const key = (category || '').toLowerCase().trim()\n   129→  const type = CATEGORY_TO_PRODUCT_TYPE[key] || 'tshirt'\n   130→  return CSS_PREVIEW_ZONES[type] || CSS_PREVIEW_ZONES['tshirt']\n   131→}\n   132→\n   133→// ── Unified personalization rendering constants ────────────────────────\n   134→\n   135→/** Consistent line height across preview, server-render, and production */\n   136→export const LINE_HEIGHT = 1.3\n   137→\n   138→/** Font size ratios relative to a base size (medium = 1.0) */\n   139→export const FONT_SIZE_RATIOS = { small: 0.67, medium: 1.0, large: 1.5 } as const\n   140→\n   141→/** Compute pixel font size from a preset and base size */\n   142→export function computeFontSize(preset: 'small' | 'medium' | 'large', baseSize: number): number {\n   143→  return Math.round(baseSize * FONT_SIZE_RATIOS[preset])\n   144→}\n   145→\n   146→/**\n   147→ * Production print dimensions per product 151→  'tshirt':     { w: 3600, h: 4800 },\n   152→  'hoodie':     { w: 3000, h: 3600 },\n   153→  'mug':        { w: 2850, h: 1050 },\n   154→  'phone-case': { w: 750,  h: 1500 },\n   155→  'tote-bag':   { w: 3600, h: 3600 },\n   156→  'hat':        { w: 1650, h: 750 },\n   157→}\n   158→\n   159→/**\n   160→ * Production font size ratios — medium = 8% of placeholder height.\n   161→ * Use with computeFontSize() for production-resolution text rendering.\n   162→ */\n   163→export function getProductionBaseFontSize(productType: string): number {\n   164→  const dims = PRODUCTION_DIMENSIONS[productType] || PRODUCTION_DIMENSIONS['tshirt']\n   165→  return Math.round(dims.h * 0.08)\n   166→}\n   167→\n   168→/**\n   169→ * Get the real aspect ratio (width/height) for a product type.\n   170→ * Derived from PRODUCTION_DIMENSIONS which match actual print specs.\n   171→ */\n   172→export function getProductAspectRatio(productType: string, panelId: string = 'front'): number {\n   173→  // Check panel-specific overrides first\n   174→  const panelAR = PANEL_ASPECT_RATIOS[productType]?.[panelId]\n   175→  if (panelAR) return panelAR\n   176→\n   177→  const dims = } from 'canvas'\n    11→import path from 'path'\n    12→import fs from 'fs'\n    13→import { PRINT_AREAS, PRODUCTION_DIMENSIONS, PrintArea } from '@/lib/print-areas'\n    14→import { supabaseAdmin }  33→let fontsRegistered = false\n    34→\n    35→function ensureFontsRegistered(): void {\n    36→  if (fontsRegistered) return\n    37→  Object.entries(FONT_FILES).forEach(([fontName, fileName]) => {\n    38→    const fontPath = path.join(process.cwd(), 'public', 'fonts', fileName)\n    39→    if (fs.existsSync(fontPath)) {\n    40→      try {\n    41→        registerFont(fontPath, { family: fontName })\n    42→      } catch {\n    43→        // Font may already be registered\n    44→      }\n    45→    }\n    46→  })\n    47→  fontsRegistered = true\n    48→}\n    49→\n    50→// ── Layer types ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    51→\n    52→export interface CompositionLayer {\n    53→  type: 'text' | 'image' | 'ai'\n    54→  url?: string\n    55→  text?: string\n    56→  font?: string\n    57→  color?: string\n    58→  fontSize?: string // 'small' | 'medium' | 'large' or pixel value\n    59→  position?: string // 'top' | 'center' | 'bottom'\n    60→  textAlign?: string // 'left' | 'center' | 'right'\n    61→}\n    62→\n    63→// ── Position mapping ─────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    64→\n    65→const POSITION_Y_RATIO: Record<string, number> = {\n    66→  top: 0.15,\n    67→  center: 0.5,\n    68→  bottom: 0.85,\n    69→}\n    70→\n    71→// ── Font size parsing ────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    72→\n    73→function parseFontSize(fontSize: string | undefined, baseHeight: number): number {\n    74→  if (!fontSize) return Math.round(baseHeight * 0.08) // default: 8% of height\n    75→  if (fontSize === 'small') return Math.round(baseHeight * 0.05)\n    76→  if (fontSize === 'medium') return Math.round(baseHeight * 0.08)\n    77→  if (fontSize === 'large') return Math.round(baseHeight * 0.12)\n    78→  // Try parsing as numeric pixel value\n    79→  const parsed = parseInt(fontSize, 10)\n    80→  if (!isNaN(parsed) && parsed > 0) return parsed\n    81→  return Math.round(baseHeight * 0.08)\n    82→}\n    83→\n    84→// ── Text rendering via canvas ────────────────────────────────────────\n    85→\n    86→function renderTextToBuffer(\n    87→  text: string,\n    88→  font: string,\n    89→  fontSize: number,\n    90→  color: string,\n    91→  width: number,\n    92→  height: number,\n    93→  textAlign: CanvasTextAlign = 'center',\n    94→  positionY: number = 0.5\n    95→): Buffer {\n    96→  ensureFontsRegistered()\n    97→\n    98→  const canvas = createCanvas(width, height)\n    99→  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')\n   100→\n   101→  // Transparent background\n   102→  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height)\n   103→\n   104→  // Resolve font family\n   105→  const selectedFont = FONT_FILES[font] ? font : 'Inter'\n   106→  ctx.font = `${fontSize}px \"${selectedFont}\"`\n   107→  ctx.fillStyle = color || '#000000'\n   108→  ctx.textAlign = textAlign\n   109→  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle'\n   110→\n   111→  // Calculate x position based on alignment\n   112→  let x: number\n   113→  switch (textAlign) {\n   114→    case 'left':\n   115→      x = Math.round(width * 0.05) // 5% margin\n   116→      break\n   117→    case 'right':\n   118→      x = Math.round(width * 0.95)\n   119→      break\n   120→    default:\n   121→      x = Math.round(width / 2)\n   122→  }\n   123→\n   124→  // Multi-line text rendering\n   125→  const lineHeight = fontSize * 1.3\n   126→  const textLines = text.split('\\n')\n   127→  const totalTextHeight = textLines.length * lineHeight\n   128→  const startY = Math.round(height * positionY - totalTextHeight / 2 + lineHeight / 2)\n   129→\n   130→  textLines.forEach((line, index) => {\n   131→    const y = startY + index * lineHeight\n   132→    ctx.fillText(line, x, y)\n   133→  })\n   134→\n   135→  return canvas.toBuffer('image/png')\n   136→}\n   137→\n   138→// ── Preview rendering ────────────────────────────────────────────────\n   139→\n   140→/**\n   141→ * Render a multi-layer design composition as a 1024x1024 preview PNG.\n   142→ *\n   143→ * @param layers - Array of composition layers (text, image, ai)\n   144→ * @param productType - Product type key (tshirt, hoodie, mug, etc.)\n   145→ * @returns PNG buffer of the composed preview\n   146→ */\n   147→export async function renderCompositionPreview(\n   148→  layers: CompositionLayer[],\n   149→  productType: string\n   150→): Promise<Buffer> {\n   151→  const CANVAS_SIZE = 1024\n   152→  const area: PrintArea = PRINT_AREAS[productType] || PRINT_AREAS['tshirt']\n   153→\n   154→  // Start with a transparent 1024x1024 canvas\n   155→  let composite = sharp({\n   156→    create: {\n   157→      width: CANVAS_SIZE,\n   158→      height: CANVAS_SIZE,\n   159→      channels: 4,\n   160→      background: { r: 0, g: 0, b: 0, alpha: 0 },\n   161→    },\n   162→  }).png()\n   163→\n   164→  const compositeInputs: sharp.OverlayOptions[] = []\n   165→\n   166→  for (const layer of layers) {\n   167→    try {\n   168→      if (layer.type === 'image' || layer.type === 'ai') {\n   169→        if (!layer.url) continue\n   170→\n   171→        // Fetch the image\n   172→        const response = await fetch(layer.url)\n   173→        if (!response.ok) {\n   174→          console.error(`Failed to fetch layer image: ${response.status} ${layer.url}`)\n   175→          continue\n   176→        }\n   177→        const imageBuffer = Buffer.from(await response.arrayBuffer())\n   178→\n   179→        // Resize to fit print area\n   180→        const resized = await sharp(imageBuffer)\n   181→ .toBuffer()\n   187→\n   188→        compositeInputs.push({\n   189→          input: resized,\n   190→          left: area.x,\n   191→          top: area.y,\n   192→          blend: 'over',\n   193→        })\n   194→      } else if (layer.type === 'text') {\n   195→        if (!layer.text) continue\n   196→\n   197→        const fontSize = parseFontSize(layer.fontSize, area.h)\n   198→        const positionY = POSITION_Y_RATIO[layer.position || 'center'] ?? 0.5\n   199→        const textAlign = (layer.textAlign as CanvasTextAlign) || 'center'\n   200→\n   201→        const textBuffer = renderTextToBuffer(\n   202→          layer.text,\n   203→          layer.font || 'Inter',\n   204→          fontSize,\n   205→          layer.color || '#000000',\n   206→          area.w,\n   207→ area.x,\n   215→          top: area.y,\n   216→          blend: 'over',\n   217→        })\n   218→      }\n   219→    } catch (layerError) {\n   220→      console.error('Error processing composition layer:', layerError)\n   221→      // Continue with remaining layers\n   222→    }\n   223→  }\n   224→\n   225→  if (compositeInputs.length > 0) {\n   226→    composite = composite.composite(compositeInputs)\n   227→  }\n   228→\n   229→  return composite.png().toBuffer()\n   230→}\n   231→\n   232→// ── Production export }\n   275→\n   276→  const layers: CompositionLayer[] = composition.layers || []\n   277→  const prodDims = PRODUCTION_DIMENSIONS[productType] || PRODUCTION_DIMENSIONS['tshirt']\n   278→  const previewArea.w\n   282→  const scaleY = prodDims.h / previewArea.h\n   283→\n   284→  // Production canvas is just the print area at production resolution\n   285→  const prodWidth = prodDims.w\n 293→      background: { r: 0, g: 0, b: 0, alpha: 0 },\n   294→    },\n   295→  }).png()\n   296→\n   297→  const compositeInputs: sharp.OverlayOptions[] = []\n   298→\n   299→  for (const layer of layers) {\n   300→    try {\n   301→      if (layer.type === 'image' || layer.type === 'ai') {\n   302→        if (!layer.url) continue\n   303→\n   304→        const response = await b: 0, alpha: 0 },\n   313→          })\n   314→          .png()\n   315→          .toBuffer()\n   316→\n   317→        compositeInputs.push({\n   318→          input: resized,\n   319→          left: Scale font size to production resolution\n   327→        const previewFontSize = parseFontSize(layer.fontSize, previewArea.h)\n   328→        const prodFontSize = Math.round(previewFontSize * scaleY)\n   329→        const positionY = POSITION_Y_RATIO[layer.position || 'center'] ?? 0.5\n   330→        const textAlign = (layer.textAlign as CanvasTextAlign) || 'center'\n   331→\n   332→     1→/**\n     2→ * Design Composition API\n     3→ *\n     4→ * POST /api/designs/compose\n     5→ * Creates a design composition from multiple layers, generates a preview,\n     6→ * and stores the composition in the database.\n     7→ */\n     8→\n     9→import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\n    10→import { cookies } from 'next/headers'\n    11→import { supabaseAdmin } from '@/lib/supabase-admin'\n    12→import { renderCompositionPreview, type CompositionLayer } from '@/lib/composition-renderer'\n    13→\n    14→export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {\n    15→  try {\n    16→    // ── Auth ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    17→    const cookieStore = await cookies()\n    18→    const token = cookieStore.get('sb-access-token')?.value\n    19→    if (!token) {\n    20→      return NextResponse.json(\n    21→        { error: 'Authentication required' },\n    22→        { status: 401 }\n    23→      )\n    24→    }\n    25→\n    26→    const { data: { user } } = await supabaseAdmin.auth.getUser(token)\n    27→    if (!user) {\n    28→      return NextResponse.json(\n    29→        { error: 'Authentication required' },\n    30→        { status: 401 }\n    31→      )\n    32→    }\n    33→\n    34→    // ── Parse body ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    35→    const body = await request.json()\n    36→    const {\n    37→      layers,\n    38→      product_type,\n    39→      product_id,\n    40→    }: {\n    41→      layers: CompositionLayer[]\n    42→      product_type: string\n    43→      product_id?: string\n    44→    } = body\n    45→\n    46→    // ── Validate ────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    47→    if (!layers || !Array.isArray(layers) || layers.length === 0) {\n    48→      return NextResponse.json(\n    49→        { error: 'At least one layer is required' },\n    50→        { status: 400 }\n    51→      )\n    52→    }\n    53→\n    54→    if (!product_type || typeof product_type !== 'string') {\n    55→      return NextResponse.json(\n    56→        { error: 'product_type is required' },\n    57→        { status: 400 }\n    58→      )\n    59→    }\n    60→\n    61→    // Validate layer structure\n    62→    for (let i = 0; i < layers.length; i++) {\n    63→      const layer = layers[i]\n    64→      if (!layer.type || !['text', 'image', 'ai'].includes(layer.type)) {\n    65→        return NextResponse.json(\n    66→          { error: `Invalid layer type at index ${i}: must be 'text', 'image', or 'ai'` },\n    67→          { status: 400 }\n    68→        )\n    69→      }\n    70→      if ((layer.type === 'image' || layer.type === 'ai') && !layer.url) {\n    71→        return NextResponse.json(\n    72→          { error: `Layer ${i} (${layer.type}) requires a url` },\n    73→          { status: 400 }\n    74→        )\n    75→      }\n    76→      if (layer.type === 'text' && !layer.text) {\n    77→        return NextResponse.json(\n    78→          { error: `Layer ${i} (text) requires text content` },\n    79→          { status: 400 }\n    80→        )\n    81→      }\n    82→    }\n    83→\n    84→    // Validate product_id exists if provided\n    85→    if (product_id) {\n    86→      const { data: product } = await supabaseAdmin\n    87→        .from('products')\n    88→        .select('id')\n    89→        .eq('id', product_id)\n    90→        .is('deleted_at', null)\n    91→        .single()\n    92→\n    93→      if (!product) {\n    94→        return NextResponse.json(\n    95→          { error: 'Product not found' },\n    96→          { status: 404 }\n    97→        )\n    98→      }\n    99→    }\n   100→\n   101→    // ── Render preview ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n   102→    const previewBuffer = await renderCompositionPreview(layers, product_type)\n   103→\n   104→    // ── Upload preview to Supabase Storage ──────────────────────────\n   105→    const compositionId = crypto.randomUUID()\n   106→    const filename = `compositions/${compositionId}/preview.png`\n   107→    let previewUrl: string\n   108→\n   109→    const { error: uploadError } = await supabaseAdmin.storage\n   110→      .from('designs')\n   111→      .upload(filename, previewBuffer, {\n   112→        contentType: 'image/png',\n   113→        cacheControl: '3600',\n   114→      })\n   115→\n   116→    if (uploadError) {\n   117→      console.error('Preview upload error:', uploadError)\n   118→      // Fallback to base64 data URL\n   119→      const base64 = previewBuffer.toString('base64')\n   120→      previewUrl = `data:image/png;base64,${base64}`\n   121→    } else {\n   122→      const { data: { publicUrl } } = supabaseAdmin.storage\n   123→        .from('designs')\n   124→        .getPublicUrl(filename)\n   125→      previewUrl = publicUrl\n   126→    }\n   127→\n   128→    // ── Create DB row ───────────────────────────────────────────────\n   129→    const { data: composition, error: insertError } = await supabaseAdmin\n   130→      .from('design_compositions')\n   131→      .insert({\n   132→        id: compositionId,\n   133→        user_id: user.id,\n   134→        product_type,\n   135→        product_id: product_id || null,\n   136→        schema_version: 1,\n   137→        layers,\n   138→        preview_url: previewUrl,\n   139→        status: 'draft',\n   140→      })\n   141→      .select('id, preview_url, status, created_at')\n   142→      .single()\n   143→\n   144→    if (insertError) {\n   145→      console.error('Composition insert error:', insertError)\n   146→      return NextResponse.json(\n   147→        { error: 'Failed to save composition', message: insertError.message },\n   148→        { status: 500 }\n   149→      )\n   150→    }\n   151→\n   152→    return NextResponse.json({\n   153→      success: true,\n   154→      composition_id: composition.id,\n   155→      preview_url: composition.preview_url,\n   156→    })\n   157→  } catch (error) {\n   158→    console.error('Designs compose API error:', error)\n   159→    return NextResponse.json(\n   160→      { error: 'Internal server error' },\n   161→      { status: 500 }\n   162→    )\n   163→  }\n   164→}\n   165→"}]},"uuid":"39d85175-2985-40db-b813-16442605ee5c","timestamp":"2026-03-05T18:42:19.388Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"89826d61-f56d-4db4-a614-7515afc03a64"}

    1→import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\n     2→import { supabaseAdmin } from '@/lib/supabase-admin'\n     3→import { createServerClient } from '@/lib/supabase-server'\n     4→\n     5→export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {\n     6→  try {\n     7→    // Authenticate user\n     8→    const { user } = await createServerClient(request)\n     9→    if (!user) {\n    10→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 })\n    11→    }\n    12→\n    13→    const body = await request.json()\n    14→    const { fabricJson, previewDataUrl, productType, productId, compositionId } = body\n    15→\n    16→    if (!fabricJson || !productId) {\n    17→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Missing required fields' }, { status: 400 })\n    18→    }\n    19→\n    20→    const id = compositionId || crypto.randomUUID()\n    21→\n    22→    // If updating existing composition, verify ownership\n    23→    if (compositionId) {\n    24→      const { data: existing } = await supabaseAdmin\n    25→        .from('design_compositions')\n    26→        .select('user_id')\n    27→        .eq('id', compositionId)\n    28→        .single()\n    29→      if (existing && existing.user_id !== user.id) {\n    30→        return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Forbidden' }, { status: 403 })\n    31→      }\n    32→    }\n    33→\n    34→    // Upload preview image to Supabase Storage\n    35→    let previewUrl: string | null = null\n    36→    if (previewDataUrl && previewDataUrl.startsWith('data:image/png;base64,')) {\n    37→      const base64Data = previewDataUrl.replace(/^data:image\\/png;base64,/, '')\n    38→      const buffer = Buffer.from(base64Data, 'base64')\n    39→\n    40→      const filename = `compositions/${id}/preview.png`\n    41→      const { error: uploadError } = await supabaseAdmin.storage\n    42→        .from('designs')\n    43→        .upload(filename, buffer, {\n    44→          contentType: 'image/png',\n    45→          cacheControl: '31536000',\n    46→          upsert: true,\n    47→        })\n    48→\n    49→      if (!uploadError) {\n    50→        const { data: { publicUrl } } = supabaseAdmin.storage\n    51→          .from('designs')\n    52→          .getPublicUrl(filename)\n    53→        previewUrl = publicUrl\n    54→      }\n    55→    }\n    56→\n    57→    // Upsert composition record\n    58→    const { error: dbError } = await supabaseAdmin\n    59→      .from('design_compositions')\n    60→      .upsert({\n    61→        id,\n    62→        user_id: user.id,\n    63→        schema_version: 2,\n    64→        layers: fabricJson,\n    65→        product_type: productType || 'tshirt',\n    66→        product_id: productId,\n    67→        preview_url: previewUrl,\n    68→        status: 'draft',\n    69→        updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n    70→      }, { onConflict: 'id' })\n    71→\n    72→    if (dbError) {\n    73→      console.error('Failed to save composition:', dbError)\n    74→      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Database error' }, { status: 500 })\n    75→    }\n    76→\n    77→    return NextResponse.json({\n    78→      success: true,\n    79→      composition_id: id,\n    80→      preview_url: previewUrl,\n    81→    })\n    82→  } catch (error) {\n    83→    console.error('compose-v2 error:', error)\n    84→    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Internal server error' }, { status: 500 })\n    85→  }\n    86→}\n    87→"}]},"uuid":"647b73db-acba-4e43-8582-9db037515925","timestamp":"2026-03-05T18:42:19.455Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"38d9f01a-450c-429b-a833-51d1c1614ece"}

    1→/**\n     2→ * Stripe Checkout Session Creation API\n     3→ *\n     4→ * POST /api/checkout/create-session\n     5→ * Creates a Stripe Checkout session for payment processing\n     6→ */\n     7→\n     8→import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';\n     9→import { stripe } from '@/lib/stripe';\n    10→import { supabaseAdmin } from '@/lib/supabase-admin';\n    11→import { getProvider, initializeProviders } from '@/lib/pod';\n    12→import { STORE_DEFAULTS, ALLOWED_SHIPPING_COUNTRIES, BASE_URL } from '@/lib/store-config';\n    13→import { LINE_HEIGHT, computeFontSize, getProductionBaseFontSize, CATEGORY_TO_PRODUCT_TYPE } from '@/lib/print-areas';\n    14→import { createCanvas, registerFont } from 'canvas';\n    15→import path from 'path';\n    16→import fs from 'fs';\n    17→\n    18→// Font mapping for personalization\n    19→const FONT_FILES: Record<string, string> = {\n    20→  'Inter': 'Inter-Regular.ttf',\n    21→  'Roboto': 'Roboto-Regular.ttf',\n    22→  'Playfair Display': 'PlayfairDisplay-Regular.ttf',\n    23→  'Montserrat': 'Montserrat-Regular.ttf',\n    24→  'Oswald': 'Oswald-Regular.ttf',\n    25→  'Lato': 'Lato-Regular.ttf',\n    26→  'Pacifico': 'Pacifico-Regular.ttf',\n    27→  'Dancing Script': 'DancingScript-Regular.ttf',\n    28→  'Great Vibes': 'GreatVibes-Regular.ttf',\n    29→  'Caveat': 'Caveat-Regular.ttf',\n    30→  'Permanent Marker': 'PermanentMarker-Regular.ttf',\n    31→  'Bebas Neue': 'BebasNeue-Regular.ttf',\n    32→};\n    33→\n    34→// Register all fonts once at module load\n    35→Object.entries(FONT_FILES).forEach(([fontName, fileName]) => {\n    36→  const fontPath = path.join(process.cwd(), 'public', 'fonts', fileName);\n    37→  if (fs.existsSync(fontPath)) {\n    38→    registerFont(fontPath, { family: fontName });\n    39→  }\n    40→});\n    41→\n    42→/**\n    43→ * Generate a high-resolution PNG with personalization text\n    44→ * Returns base64 string (without data:image/png;base64, prefix)\n    45→ */\n    46→function generatePersonalizationPNG(\n    47→  text: string,\n    48→  font: string,\n    49→  fontSize: number,\n    50→  fontColor: string,\n    51→  width: number,\n    52→  height: number\n    53→): string {\n    54→  const canvas = createCanvas(width, height);\n    55→  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n    56→\n    57→  // Transparent background\n    58→  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);\n    59→\n    60→  // Set font properties\n    61→  const selectedFont = FONT_FILES[font] ? font : 'Inter';\n    62→  ctx.font = `${fontSize}px \"${selectedFont}\"`;\n    63→  ctx.fillStyle = fontColor;\n    64→  ctx.textAlign = 'center';\n    65→  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';\n    66→\n    67→  // Render multi-line text\n    68→  const lineHeight = fontSize * 1.3;\n    69→  const textLines = text.split('\\n');\n    70→  const totalHeight = textLines.length * lineHeight;\n    71→  const startY = (height / 2) - (totalHeight / 2) + (lineHeight / 2);\n    72→\n    73→  textLines.forEach((line, index) => {\n    74→    const y = startY + (index * lineHeight);\n    75→    ctx.fillText(line, width / 2, y);\n    76→  });\n    77→\n    78→  // Convert to base64 (without data URL prefix)\n    79→  const buffer = canvas.toBuffer('image/png');\n    80→  return buffer.toString('base64');\n    81→}\n    82→\n    83→/**\n    84→ * POST /api/checkout/create-session\n    85→ *\n    86→ * Create a Stripe Checkout Session for cart checkout\n    87→ *\n    88→ * Request body:\n    89→ * @param {Array} cartItems - Array of cart items with productId, variantId, quantity, personalization\n    90→ * @param {Object} shippingAddress - Shipping address (name, line1, city, state, postal_code, country)\n    91→ * @param {string} locale - User locale (en/es/de, default: en)\n    92→ * @param {string} currency - Currency code (default: EUR)\n    93→ * @param {string} customerEmail - Customer email address\n    94→ * @param {string} gift_message - Optional gift message\n    95→ *\n    96→ * @returns {Object} JSON response with Stripe Checkout Session URL\n    97→ */\n    98→export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n    99→  try {\n   100→    initializeProviders();\n   101→    const provider = getProvider();\n   102→\n   103→    const body = await req.json();\n   104→    const { cartItems, shippingAddress, locale = 'en', currency = STORE_DEFAULTS.stripeCurrency, customerEmail, gift_message, couponCode } = body;\n   105→\n   106→    // Validate required fields\n   107→    if (!cartItems || !Array.isArray(cartItems) || cartItems.length === 0) {\n   108→      return NextResponse.json(\n   109→        { error: 'Cart items are required' },\n   110→        { status: 400 }\n   111→      );\n   112→    }\n   113→\n   114→    // Validate all items have variant_id (required for Printify fulfillment)\n   115→    const itemsWithoutVariant = cartItems.filter(\n   116→      (item: any) => !item.variant_id\n   117→    );\n   118→    if (itemsWithoutVariant.length > 0) {\n   119→      return NextResponse.json(\n   120→        {\n   121→          error: 'Incomplete cart items',\n   122→          code: 'MISSING_VARIANTS',\n   123→          message: 'All items require a variant selection (size/color).',\n   124→          items: itemsWithoutVariant.map((i: any) => i.product_id),\n   125→        },\n   126→        { status: 400 }\n   127→      );\n   128→    }\n   129→\n   130→    // Validate all products exist and are active\n   131→    const productIds = [...new Set(cartItems.map((item: any) => item.product_id).filter(Boolean))];\n   132→\n   133→    // Verify all products are active\n   134→    if (productIds.length > 0) {\n   135→      const { data: activeProducts } = await supabaseAdmin\n   136→        .from('products')\n   137→        .select('id')\n   138→        .in('id', productIds)\n   139→        .eq('status', 'active')\n   140→        .is('deleted_at', null);\n   141→\n   142→      const activeProductIds = new Set((activeProducts || []).map((p: any) => p.id));\n   143→      const inactiveItems = productIds.filter(id => !activeProductIds.has(id));\n   144→      if (inactiveItems.length > 0) {\n   145→        return NextResponse.json(\n   146→          {\n   147→            error: 'Some products are no longer available',\n   148→            code: 'PRODUCTS_UNAVAILABLE',\n   149→            items: inactiveItems,\n   150→          },\n   151→          { status: 409 }\n   152→        );\n   153→      }\n   154→    }\n   155→\n   156→    // Stock validation: check variant availability before creating payment session\n   157→    if (productIds.length > 0) {\n   158→      const { data: variants } = await supabaseAdmin\n   159→        .from('product_variants')\n   160→        .select('product_id, color, size, is_available, price_cents')\n   161→        .in('product_id', productIds)\n   162→        .eq('is_enabled', true);\n   163→\n   164→      const unavailableItems: Array<{ productId: string; color?: string; size?: string; name?: string }> = [];\n   165→\n   166→      for (const item of cartItems) {\n   167→        if (!item.product_id) continue;\n   168→        const itemColor = item.variant_details?.color || item.color;\n   169→        const itemSize = item.variant_details?.size || item.size;\n   170→\n   171→        // Find matching variant\n   172→        const matchingVariant = variants?.find(v =>\n   173→          v.product_id === item.product_id &&\n   174→          (!itemColor || v.color === itemColor) &&\n   175→          (!itemSize || v.size === itemSize)\n   176→        );\n   177→\n   178→        // If no variant found or variant is unavailable\n   179→        if (!matchingVariant || !matchingVariant.is_available) {\n   180→          unavailableItems.push({\n   181→            productId: item.product_id,\n   182→            color: itemColor,\n   183→            size: itemSize,\n   184→            name: item.product_name || item.name,\n   185→          });\n   186→        }\n   187→      }\n   188→\n   189→      if (unavailableItems.length > 0) {\n   190→        return NextResponse.json(\n   191→          {\n   192→            error: 'ITEMS_UNAVAILABLE',\n   193→            unavailableItems,\n   194→          },\n   195→          { status: 409 }\n   196→        );\n   197→      }\n   198→    }\n   199→\n   200→    // --- Coupon validation ---"}]},"uuid":"7ad8d839-67bf-4d9a-aa8f-9af03280b842","timestamp":"2026-03-05T18:42:21.602Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"69cfc8c4-6bd7-412a-af31-1f1cb9d8c095"}

    1→import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\n     2→import { cookies } from 'next/headers'\n     3→import { supabaseAdmin } from '@/lib/supabase-admin'\n     4→import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\n     5→import { STORE_DEFAULTS } from '@/lib/store-config'\n     6→\n     7→const MAX_CART_QUANTITY = STORE_DEFAULTS.maxCartQuantity\n     8→\n     9→// GET /api/cart - Fetch cart items\n    10→export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {\n    11→  try {\n    12→    const cookieStore = await cookies()\n    13→    const sessionCookie = cookieStore.get('sb-access-token')\n    14→\n    15→    // Get session ID for guest carts (fallback)\n    16→    let sessionId = cookieStore.get('cart-session-id')?.value\n    17→    let needsSessionCookie = false\n    18→    if (!sessionId) {\n    19→      sessionId = crypto.randomUUID()\n    20→      needsSessionCookie = true\n    21→    }\n    22→\n    23→    const supabase = supabaseAdmin\n    24→\n    25→    // Try to get user from session\n    26→    let userId: string | null = null\n    27→    let userScopedClient: ReturnType<typeof createClient> | null = null\n    28→    if (sessionCookie) {\n    29→      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser(sessionCookie.value)\n    30→      userId = user?.id || null\n    31→\n    32→      // Create user-scoped client for RLS-protected queries (personalizations)\n    33→      if (userId) {\n    34→        userScopedClient = createClient(\n    35→          process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,\n    36→          process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,\n    37→          {\n    38→            auth: { persistSession: false },\n    39→            global: { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${sessionCookie.value}` } },\n    40→          }\n    41→        )\n    42→      }\n    43→    }\n    44→\n    45→    // Fetch cart items (either by user_id or session_id)\n    46→    const query = supabase\n    47→      .from('cart_items')\n    48→      .select('id, product_id, quantity, variant_id, personalization_id, composition_id, created_at, updated_at')\n    49→      .order('created_at', {"parentUuid":"9420f562-ed45-47fc-bcf5-67395bf403a4","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project/frontend","sessionId":"395964ae-6f7f-4fea-bbec-d5524241999b","version":"2.1.66","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"a8b12386dfe1d2a07","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01PfvBvGxE2MxaUahbHwiUhk","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→/**\n     2→ * Stripe Webhook Handler\n     3→ *\n     4→ * POST /api/webhooks/stripe\n     5→ * Handles Stripe webhook events (checkout.session.completed, etc.)\n     6→ */\n     7→\n     8→import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\n     9→import { headers } from 'next/headers'\n    10→import { stripe } from '@/lib/stripe'\n    11→import { getProvider, initializeProviders } from '@/lib/pod'\n    12→import { canonicalAddressFromStripe } from '@/lib/pod/printify/mapper'\n    13→import { sendOrderConfirmationEmail, sendCreditPurchaseEmail } from '@/lib/resend'\n    14→import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\n    15→import { triggerDripSequence } from '@/lib/email-drip'\n    16→import { BASE_URL } from '@/lib/store-config'\n    17→import Stripe from 'stripe'\n    18→\n    19→// Initialize Supabase client with service role key for webhook\n    20→const supabase = createClient(\n    21→  process.env.SUPABASE_URL!,\n    22→  process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY!\n    23→)\n    24→\n    25→export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n    26→  const body = await req.text()\n    27→  const headersList = await headers()\n    28→  const signature = headersList.get('stripe-signature')\n    29→\n    30→  if (!signature) {\n    31→    return NextResponse.json(\n    32→      { error: 'Missing stripe-signature header' },\n    33→      { status: 400 }\n    34→    )\n    35→  }\n    36→\n    37→  let event: Stripe.Event\n    38→\n    39→  try {\n    40→    // Verify webhook signature\n    41→    event = stripe.webhooks.constructEvent(\n    42→      body,\n    43→      signature,\n    44→      process.env.STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET!\n    45→    )\n    46→  } catch (err) {\n    47→    console.error('Webhook signature verification failed:', err)\n    48→    return NextResponse.json(\n    49→      { error: 'Webhook signature verification failed' },\n    50→      { status: 400 }\n    51→    )\n    52→  }\n    53→\n    54→  // Handle the event\n    55→  switch (event.type) {\n    56→    case 'checkout.session.completed':\n    57→      await handleCheckoutSessionCompleted(event.data.object as Stripe.Checkout.Session)\n    58→      break\n    59→\n    60→    case 'customer.subscription.created':\n    61→    case 'customer.subscription.updated':\n    62→      await handleSubscriptionUpdate(event.data.object as Stripe.Subscription)\n    63→      break\n    64→\n    65→    case 'customer.subscription.deleted':\n    66→      await handleSubscriptionDeleted(event.data.object as Stripe.Subscription)\n    67→      break\n    68→\n    69→    case 'payment_intent.succeeded':\n    70→      console.log('PaymentIntent succeeded:', event.data.object.id)\n    71→      break\n    72→\n    73→    case 'payment_intent.payment_failed':\n    74→      console.log('PaymentIntent failed:', event.data.object.id)\n    75→      break\n    76→\n    77→    case 'invoice.payment_failed':\n    78→      await handleInvoicePaymentFailed(event.data.object as Stripe.Invoice)\n    79→      break\n    80→\n    81→    case 'charge.dispute.created':\n    82→      await handleChargeDisputeCreated(event.data.object as Stripe.Dispute)\n    83→      break\n    84→\n    85→    default:\n    86→      console.log(`Unhandled event type: ${event.type}`)\n    87→  }\n    88→\n    89→  return NextResponse.json({ received: true })\n    90→}\n    91→\n    92→/**\n    93→ * Handle checkout.session.completed event\n    94→ * Creates an order in the database when payment is successful\n    95→ */\n    96→async function handleCheckoutSessionCompleted(session: Stripe.Checkout.Session) {\n    97→  try {\n    98→    console.log('Processing checkout.session.completed:', session.id)\n    99→\n   100→    // Only process paid sessions\n   101→    if (session.payment_status !== 'paid') {\n   102→      console.log('Session not paid yet:', session.id)\n   103→      return\n   104→    }\n   105→\n   106→    // Extract metadata\n   107→    const locale = session.metadata?.locale || 'en'\n   108→    const cartItemsStr = session.metadata?.cart_items || '[]'\n   109→    const cartItems = JSON.parse(cartItemsStr)\n   110→    const giftMessage = session.metadata?.gift_message || null\n   111→\n   112→    // Get session details with line items and payment method\n   113→    const fullSession = await stripe.checkout.sessions.retrieve(session.id, {\n   114→      expand: ['line_items', 'payment_intent', 'payment_intent.payment_method'],\n   115→    })\n   116→\n   117→    const lineItems = fullSession.line_items?.data || []\n   118→\n   119→    // Get customer email\n   120→    const customerEmail = session.customer_details?.email || session.customer_email\n   121→\n   122→    // Build shipping address JSONB\n   123→    const shipping = session.collected_information?.shipping_details\n   124→    const shippingAddress = shipping?.address\n   125→      ? {\n   126→          name: shipping.name,\n   127→          line1: shipping.address.line1,\n   128→          line2: shipping.address.line2,\n   129→          city: shipping.address.city,\n   130→          state: shipping.address.state,\n   131→          postal_code: shipping.address.postal_code,\n   132→          country: shipping.address.country,\n   133→        }\n   134→      : null\n   135→\n   136→    // Get payment intent ID and payment method\n   137→    const paymentIntentId =\n   138→      typeof fullSession.payment_intent === 'string'\n   139→        ? fullSession.payment_intent\n   140→        : fullSession.payment_intent?.id\n   141→\n   142→    // Get payment method type (card, crypto, etc.)\n   143→    let paymentMethodType: string | null = null\n   144→    if (typeof fullSession.payment_intent !== 'string' && fullSession.payment_intent?.payment_method) {\n   145→      const paymentMethod = fullSession.payment_intent.payment_method\n   146→      if (typeof paymentMethod !== 'string') {\n   147→        paymentMethodType = paymentMethod.type || null\n   148→      }\n   149→    }\n   150→\n   151→    // Idempotency check: Check if order already exists for this session\n   152→    const { data: existingOrder } = await supabase\n   153→      .from('orders')\n   154→      .select('id, status, created_at')\n   155→      .eq('stripe_session_id', session.id)\n   156→      .single()\n   157→\n   158→    if (existingOrder) {\n   159→      console.log('Order already exists for session:', session.id, '— skipping (idempotent)')\n   160→      console.log('Existing order ID:', existingOrder.id)\n   161→      return // Idempotent: order already processed\n   162→    }\n   163→\n   164→    // Look up user by email (for authenticated users)\n   165→    let userId: string | null = null\n   166→    if (customerEmail) {\n   167→      const { data: userByEmail } = await supabase\n   168→        .from('users')\n   169→        .select('id')\n   170→        .eq('email', customerEmail)\n   171→        .single()\n   172→      userId = userByEmail?.id || null\n   173→    }\n   174→\n   175→    // Create order record\n   176→    const { data: order, error: orderError } = await supabase\n   177→      .from('orders')\n   178→      .insert({\n   179→        user_id: userId,\n   180→        stripe_session_id: session.id,\n   181→        stripe_payment_intent_id: paymentIntentId,\n   182→        status: 'paid',\n   183→        total_cents: session.amount_total || 0,\n   184→        currency: session.currency || 'eur',\n   185→        shipping_address: shippingAddress,\n   186→        customer_email: customerEmail,\n   187→        locale,\n   188→        gift_message: giftMessage,\n   189→        payment_method: paymentMethodType,\n   190→        paid_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n   191→      })\n   192→      .select()\n   193→      .single()\n   194→\n   195→    if (orderError) {\n   196→      console.error('Failed to create order:', orderError)\n   197→      throw orderError\n   198→    }\n   199→\n   200→    console.log('Created order:', order.id)"}]},"uuid":"eb1290c3-1bfc-43c6-95aa-afd2a2e2fb20","timestamp":"2026-03-05T18:42:31.835Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9420f562-ed45-47fc-bcf5-67395bf403a4"}

New Printful webhooks use the unified route.\n     7→ *\n     8→ * POST /api/webhooks/printify\n     9→ * Handles Printify webhook events (order:shipped, order:delivered, etc.)\n    10→ *\n    11→ * Architecture note: PodClaw agents do NOT receive these webhooks directly.\n    12→ * This handler writes all state changes to Supabase (orders, products,\n    13→ * notifications, audit_log), and PodClaw agents read from Supabase during\n    14→ * their scheduled runs / heartbeat cycles. This avoids coupling between\n    15→ * the frontend and PodClaw deployments.\n    16→ */\n    17→\n    18→import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\n    19→import { createHmac, timingSafeEqual } from 'crypto'\n    20→import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\n    21→import { sendOrderShippedEmail, sendOrderCancelledEmail, sendOrderDeliveredEmail, sendOrderFailedEmail } from '@/lib/resend'\n    22→import { getProvider, initializeProviders } from '@/lib/pod'\n    23→import { syncProductFromPrintify, deleteProductCascade } from '@/lib/printify-sync'\n    24→import { issueRefund } from '@/lib/reliability/refund-guard'\n    25→import { transition } from '@/lib/reliability/state-transition'\n    26→\n    27→const supabase = createClient(\n    28→  process.env.SUPABASE_URL!,\n    29→  process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY!\n    30→)\n    31→\n    32→/**\n    33→ * Verifies Printify webhook HMAC signature using constant-time comparison.\n    34→ * Uses timingSafeEqual() to prevent timing attacks.\n    35→ */\n    36→function verifyPrintifyWebhook(body: string, signature: string, secret: string): boolean {\n    37→  const hmac = createHmac('sha256', secret)\n    38→  hmac.update(body)\n    39→  const expected = hmac.digest('base64')\n    40→\n    41→  // Check length first (fast fail for obviously wrong signatures)\n    42→  if (signature.length !== expected.length) {\n    43→    return false\n    44→  }\n    45→\n    46→  // Use constant-time comparison to prevent timing attacks\n    47→  try {\n    48→    return timingSafeEqual(Buffer.from(signature), Buffer.from(expected))\n    49→  } catch {\n    50→    // timingSafeEqual throws if buffer lengths don't match\n    51→    return false\n    52→  }\n    53→}\n    54→\n    55→export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n    56→  const body = await req.text()\n    57→  const signature = req.headers.get('X-Printify-Hmac-SHA256')\n    58→\n    59→  if (!signature) {\n    60→    return NextResponse.json(\n    61→      { error: 'Missing X-Printify-Hmac-SHA256 header' },\n    62→      { status: 401 }\n    63→    )\n    64→  }\n    65→\n    66→  const webhookSecret = process.env.PRINTIFY_WEBHOOK_SECRET\n    67→  if (!webhookSecret) {\n    68→    console.error('PRINTIFY_WEBHOOK_SECRET not configured')\n    69→    return NextResponse.json(\n    70→      { error: 'Webhook secret not configured' },\n    71→      { status: 500 }\n    72→    )\n    73→  }\n    74→\n    75→  if (!verifyPrintifyWebhook(body, signature, webhookSecret)) {\n    76→    console.error('Printify webhook signature verification failed')\n    77→    return NextResponse.json(\n    78→      { error: 'Invalid signature' },\n    79→      { status: 401 }\n    80→    )\n    81→  }\n    82→\n    83→  let event: { type: string; resource: Record<string, unknown> }\n    84→  try {\n    85→    event = JSON.parse(body)\n    86→  } catch {\n    87→    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid JSON body' }, { status: 400 })\n    88→  }\n    89→\n    90→  const { type, resource } = event\n    91→\n    92→  initializeProviders()\n    93→\n    94→  try {\n    95→    switch (type) {\n    96→      case 'order:created':\n    97→        await handleOrderCreated(resource)\n    98→        break\n    99→      case 'order:shipped':\n   100→        await handleOrderShipped(resource)\n   101→        break\n   102→      case 'order:delivered':\n   103→        await handleOrderDelivered(resource)\n   104→        break\n   105→      case 'order:cancelled':\n   106→        await handleOrderCancelled(resource)\n   107→        break\n   108→      case 'order:failed':\n   109→        await handleOrderFailed(resource)\n   110→        break\n   111→      case 'product:publish:started': {\n   112→        const publishProductId = resource?.id as string\n   113→        if (publishProductId) {\n   114→          // Find the Supabase product to get external ID for publishing confirmation\n   115→          const { data: existingProduct } = await supabase\n   116→            .from('products')\n   117→            .select('id')\n   118→            .eq('printify_id', publishProductId)\n   119→            .single()\n   120→\n   121→          if (existingProduct) {\n   122→            try {\n   123→              await getProvider().confirmPublishing!(\n   124→                publishProductId,\n   125→                existingProduct.id,\n   126→                `/shop/${existingProduct.id}`\n   127→              )\n e)\n   131→            }\n   132→          } else {\n   133→            console.log(\n   134→              'Product not in Supabase yet, will be confirmed by create-product pipeline:',\n   135→ 'product:created':\n   143→      case 'product:updated': {\n   144→        const productId = resource?.id as string\n   145→        if (productId) {\n   146→          const fullProduct = await getProvider().getProduct(productId)\n   147→          await syncProductFromPrintify((fullProduct._raw || fullProduct) as any, supabase)\n   148→          console.log(`Product synced (${type}):`, order is already created\n   226→      } else {\n   227→        console.log(`Created ${validOrderItems.length} order items`)\n   228→      }\n   229→    }\n   230→\n   231→    // Create notification for the user (if user_id is found)\n   232→    // For guest checkouts, we'll create a notification linked to the email\n   233→    // Note: In a real system, you'd look up user by email first\n   234→    const orderDisplayId = order.id.slice(0, 8) // Use first 8 chars of UUID as display ID\n   235→    const itemCount = validOrderItems.length\n   236→    const totalAmount = (order.total_cents / 100).toFixed(2)\n   237→    const currencyCode = order.currency.toUpperCase()\n   238→\n   239→    const notificationData = {\n   240→      type: 'order_confirmation',\n   241→      title: `Order #${orderDisplayId} Confirmed`,\n   242→      body: `Your order has been confirmed. ${itemCount} ${itemCount === 1 ? 'item' : 'items'} • Total: ${totalAmount} ${currencyCode}`,\n   243→      data: {\n   244→        order_id: order.id,\n   245→        session_id: session.id,\n   246→        total_cents: order.total_cents,\n   247→        currency: order.currency,\n   248→        item_count: itemCount,\n   249→      },\n   250→      is_read: false,\n   251→    }\n   252→\n   253→    // Create notification for authenticated user (reuse userId from earlier lookup)\n   254→    if (userId) {\n   255→      const { error: notifError } = await supabase\n   256→        .from('notifications')\n found for email - skipping notification (guest checkout)')\n   269→    }\n   270→\n   271→    // Create audit log entry\n   272→    const { error: auditError } = await supabase\n   273→      .from('audit_log')\n   274→      .insert({\n   275→        actor_type: 'webhook',\n   276→        actor_id: 'stripe_webhook',\n   277→        action: 'order_created',\n   278→        resource_type:     1→-- ============================================================\n     2→-- Phase 3: Add provider-agnostic columns to products,\n     3→-- product_variants, orders, order_items, designs, and\n     4→-- personalizations.\n     5→-- All old Printify columns are preserved — no data is dropped.\n     6→-- Backfill copies existing Printify IDs into the new columns.\n     7→-- ============================================================\n     8→\n     9→BEGIN;\n    10→\n    11→-- -----------------------------------------------\n    12→-- products: add pod_provider + provider_product_id\n    13→-- -----------------------------------------------\n    14→ALTER TABLE products\n    15→  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS pod_provider          VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'printify',\n    16→  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS provider_product_id   TEXT,\n    17→  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS product_template_id   TEXT,\n    18→  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS provider_facility_id  TEXT;\n    19→\n    20→-- Backfill from existing Printify columns\n    21→UPDATE products\n    22→SET\n    23→  provider_product_id  = printify_id,\n    24→  product_template_id  = blueprint_id::TEXT,\n    25→  provider_facility_id = print_provider_id::TEXT\n    26→WHERE printify_id IS NOT NULL;\n    27→\n    28→-- Partial unique index on (pod_provider, provider_product_id)\n    29→-- so the upsert pattern works regardless of provider.\n    30→CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_products_provider_product_unique\n    31→  ON products (pod_provider, provider_product_id)\n    32→  WHERE provider_product_id IS NOT NULL;\n    33→\n    34→-- Composite lookup index for provider + status filtering\n    35→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_products_pod_provider\n    36→  ON products (pod_provider, status);\n    37→\n    38→-- -----------------------------------------------\n    39→-- product_variants: add external_variant_id\n    40→-- -----------------------------------------------\n    41→ALTER TABLE product_variants\n    42→  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS external_variant_id TEXT;\n    43→\n    44→-- Backfill\n    45→UPDATE product_variants\n    46→SET external_variant_id = printify_variant_id\n    47→WHERE printify_variant_id IS NOT NULL;\n    48→\n    49→-- Unique constraint for upsert: (product_id, external_variant_id)\n    50→DO $$\n    51→BEGIN\n    52→  IF NOT EXISTS (\n    53→    SELECT 1 FROM pg_constraint\n    54→    WHERE conname = 'product_variants_provider_variant_unique'\n    55→  ) THEN\n    56→    ALTER TABLE product_variants\n    57→      ADD CONSTRAINT product_variants_provider_variant_unique\n    58→      UNIQUE (product_id, external_variant_id);\n    59→  END IF;\n    60→END $$;\n    61→\n    62→-- -----------------------------------------------\n    63→-- orders: add external_order_id + pod_* columns\n    64→-- -----------------------------------------------\n    65→ALTER TABLE orders\n    66→  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS external_order_id    TEXT,\n    67→  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS pod_provider         VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'printify',\n    68→  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS pod_cost_cents       INTEGER,\n    69→  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS pod_retry_count      INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    70→  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS pod_error            TEXT,\n    71→  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS pod_last_attempt_at  TIMESTAMPTZ;\n    72→\n    73→-- Backfill\n    74→UPDATE orders\n    75→SET\n    76→  external_order_id   = printify_order_id,\n    77→  pod_cost_cents      = printify_cost_cents,\n    78→  pod_retry_count     = printify_retry_count,\n    79→  pod_error           = printify_error,\n    80→  pod_last_attempt_at = printify_last_attempt_at\n    81→WHERE printify_order_id IS NOT NULL\n    82→   OR printify_cost_cents IS NOT NULL\n    83→   OR printify_retry_count > 0;\n    84→\n    85→-- Index for retry-queue logic (mirrors idx_orders_printify_retry)\n    86→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_orders_pod_retry\n    87→  ON orders (pod_retry_count, pod_last_attempt_at)\n    88→  WHERE pod_error IS NOT NULL AND status = 'paid';\n    89→\n    90→-- Index for webhook lookups by external_order_id\n    91→CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_orders_external_order_id\n    92→  ON orders (external_order_id)\n    93→  WHERE external_order_id IS NOT NULL;\n    94→\n    95→-- -----------------------------------------------\n    96→-- order_items: add external_line_item_id\n    97→-- -----------------------------------------------\n    98→ALTER TABLE order_items\n    99→  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS external_line_item_id TEXT;\n   100→\n   101→-- Backfill\n   102→UPDATE order_items\n   103→SET external_line_item_id = printify_line_item_id\n   104→WHERE printify_line_item_id IS NOT NULL;\n   105→\n   106→-- -----------------------------------------------\n   107→-- designs: add provider_upload_id + pod_upload_url\n   108→-- -----------------------------------------------\n   109→ALTER TABLE designs\n   110→  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS provider_upload_id TEXT,\n   111→  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS pod_upload_url     TEXT;\n   112→\n   113→-- Backfill (printify_upload_id → provider_upload_id)\n   114→UPDATE designs\n   115→SET provider_upload_id = printify_upload_id\n   116→WHERE printify_upload_id IS NOT NULL;\n   117→\n   118→-- Backfill (printify_image_url → pod_upload_url)\n   119→UPDATE designs\n   120→SET pod_upload_url = printify_image_url\n   121→WHERE printify_image_url IS NOT NULL;\n   122→\n   123→-- -----------------------------------------------\n   124→-- personalizations: add provider_temp_product_id\n   125→-- -----------------------------------------------\n   126→ALTER TABLE personalizations\n   127→  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS provider_temp_product_id TEXT;\n   128→\n   129→-- Backfill\n   130→UPDATE personalizations\n   131→SET provider_temp_product_id = printify_temp_product_id\n   132→WHERE printify_temp_product_id IS NOT NULL;\n   133→\n   134→COMMIT;\n {"parentUuid":"f2ebb41e-50f2-4ff5-ab57-981b0a52ba33","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project/frontend","sessionId":"395964ae-6f7f-4fea-bbec-d5524241999b","version":"2.1.66","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"a8b12386dfe1d2a07","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01FW3cSA6Xmk6G16z21EjGZH","type":"tool_result","content":"   240→// POST /api/cart - Add item to cart\n   241→export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {\n   242→  try {\n   243→    const cookieStore = await cookies()\n   244→    const sessionCookie = cookieStore.get('sb-access-token')\n   245→\n   246→    // Get session ID for guest carts\n   247→    let sessionId = cookieStore.get('cart-session-id')?.value\n   248→    let needsSessionCookie = false\n   249→    if (!sessionId) {\n   250→      sessionId = crypto.randomUUID()\n   251→      needsSessionCookie = true\n   252→    }\n   253→\n   254→    const supabase = supabaseAdmin\n   255→\n   256→    // Try to get user from session\n   257→    let userId: string | null = null\n   258→    if (sessionCookie) {\n   259→      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser(sessionCookie.value)\n   260→      userId = user?.id || null\n   261→    }\n   262→\n   263→    const body = await request.json()\n   264→    const { product_id, quantity, variant_details, personalization_id, composition_id } = body\n   265→\n   266→    if (!product_id || !quantity || quantity < 1) {\n   267→      return NextResponse.json(\n   268→        { error: 'Invalid request', message: 'product_id and quantity are required' },\n   269→        { status: 400 }\n   270→      )\n   271→    }\n   272→\n   273→    // Validate product exists and is active\n   274→    const { data: product } = await supabase\n   275→      .from('products')\n   276→      .select('id, status')\n   277→      .eq('id', product_id)\n   278→      .eq('status', 'active')\n   279→      .is('deleted_at', null)\n   280→      .single()\n   281→\n   282→    if (!product) {\n   283→      return NextResponse.json(\n   284→        { error: 'Product not found or unavailable', code: 'PRODUCT_NOT_FOUND' },\n   285→        { status: 404 }\n   286→      )\n   287→    }\n   288→\n   289→    // Resolve variant_id from variant_details (size/color)\n   290→    let variantId: string | null = null\n   291→    if (variant_details && (variant_details.size || variant_details.color)) {\n   292→      let variantQuery = supabase\n   293→        .from('product_variants')\n   294→        .select('id')\n   295→        .eq('product_id', product_id)\n   296→        .eq('is_enabled', true)\n   297→        .eq('is_available', true)\n   298→\n   299→      if (variant_details.size) {\n   300→        variantQuery = variantQuery.eq('size', variant_details.size)\n   301→      }\n   302→      if (variant_details.color) {\n   303→        variantQuery = variantQuery.eq('color', variant_details.color)\n   304→      }\n   305→\n   306→      const { data: matchedVariants } = await variantQuery.limit(1)\n   307→      if (matchedVariants && matchedVariants.length > 0) {\n   308→        variantId = matchedVariants[0].id\n   309→      }\n   310→    }\n   311→\n   312→    // If variant not resolved, try autoselect or reject\n   313→    if (!variantId) {\n   314→      const { data: availableVariants } = await supabase\n   315→        .from('product_variants')\n   316→        .select('id, size, color')\n   317→        .eq('product_id', product_id)\n   318→        .eq('is_enabled', true)\n   319→        .eq('is_available', true)\n   320→\n   321→      if (availableVariants && availableVariants.length === 1) {\n   322→        variantId = availableVariants[0].id\n   323→      } else if (availableVariants && availableVariants.length > 1) {\n   324→        return NextResponse.json(\n   325→          {\n   326→            error: 'Variant selection required',\n   327→            code: 'VARIANT_REQUIRED',\n   328→            message: 'This product requires selecting a size/color.',\n   329→            available_variants: availableVariants,\n   330→          },\n   331→          { status: 400 }\n   332→        )\n   333→      } else {\n   334→        return NextResponse.json(\n   335→          { error: 'No available variants for this product', code: 'NO_VARIANTS' },\n   336→          { status: 400 }\n   337→        )\n   338→      }\n   339→    }\n   340→\n   341→    // Check if item already exists in cart (same product + same variant + same personalization)\n   342→    // variantId is guaranteed non-null at this point\n   343→    const existingQuery = supabase\n   344→      .from('cart_items')\n   345→      .select('*')\n   346→      .eq('product_id', product_id)\n   347→      .eq('variant_id', variantId)\n   348→\n   349→    if (personalization_id) {\n   350→      existingQuery.eq('personalization_id', personalization_id)\n   351→    } else {\n   352→      existingQuery.is('personalization_id', null)\n   353→    }\n   354→\n   355→    if (composition_id) {\n   356→      existingQuery.eq('composition_id', composition_id)\n   357→    } else {\n   358→      existingQuery.is('composition_id', null)\n   359→    }\n   360→\n   361→    if (userId) {\n   362→      existingQuery.eq('user_id', userId)\n   363→    } else {\n   364→      existingQuery.eq('session_id', sessionId)\n   365→    }\n   366→\n   367→    const { data: existingItems } = await existingQuery\n   368→\n   369→    if (existingItems && existingItems.length > 0) {\n   370→      // Update quantity if item already exists\n   371→      const existingItem = existingItems[0]\n   372→      const newQuantity = Math.min(existingItem.quantity + quantity, MAX_CART_QUANTITY)\n   373→\n   374→      const { error: updateError } = await supabase\n   375→        .from('cart_items')\n   376→        .update({ quantity: newQuantity, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })\n   377→        .eq('id', existingItem.id)\n   378→\n   379→      if (updateError) {\n   380→        console.error('Cart update error:', updateError)\n   381→        return NextResponse.json(\n   382→          { error: 'Failed to update cart', message: updateError.message },\n   383→          { status: 500 }\n   384→        )\n   385→      }\n   386→\n   387→      const response = NextResponse.json({ success: true, updated: true })\n   388→      if (needsSessionCookie) {\n   389→        response.cookies.set('cart-session-id', sessionId, {\n   390→          httpOnly: true,\n   391→          secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production',\n   392→          sameSite: 'lax',\n   393→          maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24 * 30, // 30 days\n   394→        })\n   395→      }\n   396→      return response\n   397→    }\n   398→\n   399→    // Insert new cart item (variant_id is guaranteed non-null)\n   400→    const cartItem: any = {\n   401→      product_id,\n   402→      quantity,\n   403→      variant_id: variantId,\n   404→      session_id: userId ? null : sessionId,\n   405→      user_id: userId,\n   406→      ...(personalization_id ? { personalization_id } : {}),\n   407→      ...(composition_id ? { composition_id } : {}),\n   408→    }\n   409→\n   410→    const { error: insertError } = await supabase\n   411→      .from('cart_items')\n   412→      .insert(cartItem)\n   413→\n   414→    if (insertError) {\n   415→      console.error('Cart insert error:', insertError)\n   416→      return NextResponse.json(\n   417→        { error: 'Failed to add to cart', message: insertError.message },\n   418→        { status: 500 }\n   419→      )\n   420→    }\n   421→\n   422→    const response = NextResponse.json({ success: true, added: true })\n   423→    if (needsSessionCookie) {\n   424→      response.cookies.set('cart-session-id', sessionId, {\n   425→        httpOnly: true,\n   426→        secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production',\n   427→        sameSite: 'lax',\n   428→        maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24 * 30, // 30 days\n   429→      })\n   430→
