For regular items: use product variant from DB                          │\n│ - For composition items: use temp Printful product ID                      │\n│                                                                              │\n│ Metadata stored in Stripe session:                                         │\n│ {                                                                            │\n│   cart_items: JSON.stringify([                                             │\n│     {                                                                        │\n│       product_id,                                                           │\n│       variant_id,                                                           │\n│       composition_id,                                                       │\n│       quantity                                                              │\n│     }                                                                        │\n│   ])                                                                         │\n│ }                                                                            │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n                                    ↓\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│ STEP 8: PAYMENT COMPLETED (STRIPE WEBHOOK)                                  │\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│ Event: checkout.session.completed (payment_status = 'paid')                │\n│ File: /frontend/src/app/api/webhooks/stripe/route.ts                       │\n│ Handler: handleCheckoutSessionCompleted() (Lines 96-480)                   │\n│                                                                              │\n│ Process:                                                                    │\n│  1. Extract cart_items from session.metadata                              │\n│  2. Create orders record:                                                  │\n│     {                                                                        │\n│       id: uuid,                                                             │\n│       stripe_session_id,                                                   │\n│       status: 'paid',                                                       │\n│       total_cents,                                                          │\n│       customer_email                                                        │\n│     }                                                                        │\n│                                                                              │\n│  3. Create order_items records:                                            │\n│     ⚠️  CRITICAL BUG FOUND HERE ⚠️                                         │\n│     Location: Lines 203-213                                               │\n│                                                                              │\n│     Current code:                                                           │\n│     const orderItems = lineItems.map((item, index) => {                   │\n│       const cartItem = cartItems[index] || {}                              │\n│       return {                                                              │\n│         order_id,                                                           │\n│         product_id,                                                         │\n│         variant_id,                                                         │\n│         personalization_id,                                                │\n│         quantity,                                                           │\n│         unit_price_cents                                                    │\n│       }                                                                      │\n│     })                                                                       │\n│                                                                              │\n│     ⚠️  BUG: composition_id is NOT included in order_items!                │\n│     The design reference is LOST after order creation.                     │\n│                                                                              │\n│  4. Submit to Printful:                                                    │\n│     - Uses personalization_id for personalized items                      │\n│     - Uses regular product_id for others                                   │\n│     - Does NOT reference composition_id                                    │\n│                                                                              │\n│     ⚠️  For composed items with temp Printful product ID:                 │\n│     - The temp product ID is already embedded in item._printify_temp_id  │\n│     - But if composition fails, it won't be recoverable later             │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n                                    ↓\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│ STEP 9: SUBMIT ORDER TO PRINTFUL                                            │\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│ File: /frontend/src/app/api/webhooks/stripe/route.ts                       │\n│ Lines: 322-445                                                              │\n│                                                                              │\n│ Process:                                                                    │\n│  1. For each order item:                                                   │\n│     - If personalization_id exists: use personalizationMap[id]            │\n│     - Else: use productMap[product_id]                                     │\n│     - BUG: composition_id not checked, so composed items default to       │\n│       original product, NOT temp product with design!                      │\n│                                                                              │\n│  2. Actually, temp product ID is in item._printify_temp_id (from Step 6)  │\n│     but this is a transient field, not persisted to DB or metadata        │\n│                                                                              │\n│  3. Build Printful order:                                                  │\n│     provider.createOrder({                                                 │\n│       lineItems: [{                                                        │\n│         productExternalId,    ← This MUST be temp product for compositions │\n│         variantExternalId,                                                │\n│         quantity                                                            │\n│       }],                                                                    │\n│       shippingAddress,                                                      │\n│       ...                                                                    │\n│     })                                                                       │\n│                                                                              │\n│  4. Update order:                                                           │\n│     UPDATE orders SET                                                       │\n│       external_order_id = printful_order_id,                               │\n│       status = 'submitted'                                                 │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n                                    ↓\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│ STEP 10: PRINTFUL FULFILLMENT                                               │\n├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│ Printful processes:                                                          │\n│  1. Receives order with line item using temp product ID                    │\n│  2. Temp product already has:                                              │\n│     - Design image uploaded (from Step 6)                                  │\n│     - Print areas modified (from Step 6)                                   │\n│     - Same blueprint/facility/variants as original                         │\n│                                                                              │\n│  3. Prints with custom design (from composition export)                    │\n│  4. Ships to customer                                                      │\n│                                                                              │\n│ Webhook updates order status:                                              │\n│  - order:shipped → orders.status = 'shipped'                              │\n│  - order:delivered → orders.status = 'delivered'                          │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n---\n\n## CRITICAL BUGS & GAPS FOUND\n\n### **BUG #1: composition_id NOT persisted to order_items table**\n\n**Location**: `/frontend/src/app/api/webhooks/stripe/route.ts` (Lines 203-213)\n\n**Issue**: When creating order items from the Stripe checkout session, the code extracts `personalization_id` but **completely omits `composition_id`**:\n\n```typescript\nconst orderItems = lineItems.map((item, index) => {\n  const cartItem = cartItems[index] || {}\n  return {\n    order_id: order.id,\n    product_id: cartItem.product_id || null,\n    variant_id: cartItem.variant_id,\n    personalization_id: cartItem.personalization_id || null,  // ✓ Included\n    // ❌ composition_id is MISSING here\n    quantity: item.quantity || 1,\n    unit_price_cents: Math.round((item.amount_total || 0) / (item.quantity || 1)),\n  }\n})\n```\n\n**Impact**: \n- Design reference is lost after order creation\n- Cannot recover which design was used for which order\n- No audit trail for custom designs\n- If temp Printful product fails, no way to regenerate or retry with design\n\n**Database Gap**: `order_items` table has NO `composition_id` column (checked migrations)\n\n**Fix Required**:\n1. Add `composition_id UUID REFERENCES design_compositions(id)` column to `order_items`\n2. Update webhook to include `composition_id: cartItem.composition_id || null`\n\n---\n\n### **BUG #2: Composition export fallback to preview resolution**\n\n**Location**: `/frontend/src/lib/composition-renderer.ts` (Lines 262-273)\n\n**Issue**: For schema_version 2 (Fabric.js), if `production_url` is not set, the code falls back to `preview_url`:\n\n```typescript\nif (composition.schema_version === 2) {\n  if (composition.production_url) {\n    return composition.production_url\n  }\n  if (composition.preview_url) {\n    return composition.preview_url  // ⚠️ This is only 1024×1024!\n  }\n  throw new Error(...)\n}\n```\n\n**Impact**:\n- Preview PNG is 1024×1024 (fit-for-screen, not print-quality)\n- Gets uploaded to Printful as full-resolution design\n- Result: blurry/pixelated prints with 3600×4800 canvas stretched\n\n**Root Cause**: Client-side Fabric.js export SHOULD store production PNG in Supabase before checkout, but:\n1. No code in compose-v2 route handles client production export\n2. Client exports to `previewDataUrl` (base64) but not `productionDataUrl`\n3. composition-renderer.ts has no way to distinguish client export vs server render\n\n**Fix Required**:\n1. Update DesignStudio component to export BOTH preview and production PNGs\n2. Update compose-v2 route to accept `productionDataUrl` parameter\n3. Upload production PNG to Supabase Storage and set `production_url`\n4. Remove fallback to preview_url in composition-renderer.ts\n\n---\n\n### **GAP #3: No design validation before Printful submission**\n\n**Location**: `/frontend/src/app/api/checkout/create-session/route.ts` (Lines 369-481)\n\n**Issue**: The code attempts to:\n1. Export production image\n2. Upload to Printful\n3. Create temp product\n4. Attach to order\n\nBut **there is zero error handling for design export failures**:\n\n```typescript\ntry {\n  const { exportForProduction } = await import('@/lib/composition-renderer')\n  const productionUrl = await exportForProduction(compositionId)\n  // ... proceed\n} catch (compError) {\n  console.error(`Composition ${compositionId} Printful creation failed:`, compError)\n  // ❌ SILENT FAIL: order continues without design!\n  // ❌ Customer receives blank product\n}\n```\n\n**Impact**:\n- If design export fails (network, storage error, etc.), order proceeds silently\n- Customer receives blank product (design never attached)\n- No customer notification\n- No order status indicator\n\n**Fix Required**:\n1. Validate design_compositions row exists and has preview_url\n2. Test production export in `exportForProduction()` with actual fetch\n3. If any step fails, mark order as `status = 'requires_review'` + email admin\n4. Do NOT proceed to Printful submission if design fails\n\n---\n\n### **GAP #4: composition_id not used in order fulfillment**\n\n**Location**: `/frontend/src/app/api/webhooks/stripe/route.ts` (Lines 380-400)\n\n**Issue**: When building Printful line items, the code only checks `personalization_id`:\n\n```typescript\nconst printifyProductId = item.personalization_id\n  ? personalizationMap.get(item.personalization_id)\n  : productMap.get(item.product_id)  // ⚠️ Falls back to original product!\n```\n\n**Impact**:\n- If `composition_id` exists but `personalization_id` is null, uses original product\n- Temp Printful product with custom design is ignored\n- Order submitted without design\n\n**Root Cause**: `composition_id` is never checked here. The `item._printify_temp_id` field (set in Step 6) is transient and lost by the webhook.\n\n**Fix Required**:\n1. Persist `composition_id` to `order_items` (fixes Bug #1)\n2. In webhook, load composition metadata:\n   ```typescript\n   const compositionMap = new Map(\n     compositions.map(c => [c.id, c.provider_temp_product_id || c.printify_temp_product_id])\n   )\n   ```\n3. Check composition_id FIRST:\n   ```typescript\n   const printifyProductId = item.composition_id\n     ? compositionMap.get(item.composition_id)\n     : item.personalization_id\n       ? personalizationMap.get(item.personalization_id)\n       : productMap.get(item.product_id)\n   ```\n\n---\n\n### **GAP #5: No production dimension scaling in export**\n\n**Location**: `/frontend/src/lib/composition-renderer.ts` (Lines 280-286)\n\n**Issue**: The calculation is correct but ONLY for schema_version 1 (old layer system):\n\n```typescript\nconst scaleX = prodDims.w / previewArea.w  // 3600 / 400 = 9x\nconst scaleY = prodDims.h / previewArea.h  // 4800 / 500 = 9.6x\n\nconst prodWidth = prodDims.w   // 3600\nconst prodHeight = prodDims.h  // 4800\n```\n\nFor schema_version 2 (Fabric.js), client should handle this, but:\n- Client doesn't know PRODUCTION_DIMENSIONS\n- Client doesn't scale canvas before export\n- Results in 1024×1024 \"production\" export\n\n**Impact**: \n- Design fits entire canvas (full 1024×1024)\n- Gets squeezed into print area on final print\n- Aspect ratio may be wrong\n- Text may be cut off\n\n**Fix Required**:\n1. Import PRODUCTION_DIMENSIONS in DesignStudio component\n2. Create canvas export with `multiplier = prodDims.w / 1024`\n3. Pass production export separately to compose-v2 route\n4. Store in Supabase as production_url, NOT as preview fallback\n\n---\n\n### **GAP #6: Schema version 2 assumes client-side export handling**\n\n**Location**: `/frontend/src/lib/composition-renderer.ts` (Lines 260-274)\n\n**Issue**: Schema v2 documentation says:\n\n```typescript\n// For Fabric.js compositions, the client exports the final PNG.\n// Production export is handled client-side via canvas.toDataURL() with multiplier.\n// If a production_url is already set (from client export), return it.\n```\n\nBut **NO CODE actually implements this in the client**. The DesignStudio component:\n1. ✓ Exports preview PNG\n2. ✗ Does NOT export production PNG\n3. ✗ Does NOT pass production PNG to compose-v2 route\n4. ✗ Does NOT save production_url to database\n\n**Result**: `production_url` is always NULL for Fabric.js designs → exports fall back to preview\n\n**Fix Required**:\n1. Implement client-side production export in DesignStudio.tsx\n2. Pass productionDataUrl to /api/designs/compose-v2\n3. Upload and store as production_url in design_compositions row\n\n---\n\n## SUMMARY TABLE: DATA FLOW & STORAGE\n\n| Step | Component | Input | Output | Storage | Risk |\n|------|-----------|-------|--------|---------|------|\n| 1 | DesignStudio.tsx | Fabric canvas | preview PNG + Fabric JSON | design_compositions | ⚠️ No production export |\n| 2 | compose-v2 route | previewDataUrl | composition_id | Supabase + Storage | ✓ Safe |\n| 3 | Cart API | composition_id | cart_items row | cart_items | ✓ Safe |\n| 4 | Checkout form | cart items | metadata | Stripe session | ✓ Safe |\n| 5 | composition-renderer | design_compositions | production_url | design_compositions | ⚠️ Fallback to preview |\n| 6 | Printful API | production image | temp product ID | transient in item | ⚠️ Not persisted |\n| 7 | Stripe webhook | cart_items metadata | order + order_items | orders, order_items | 🔴 **BUG: composition_id lost** |\n| 8 | Stripe webhook | order_items | Printful order | orders.external_order_id | 🔴 **BUG: Design not linked** |\n| 9 | Printful | temp product ID | print job | Printful print queue | ⚠️ If temp ID lost → blank print |\n\n---\n\n## RECOMMENDATIONS (PRIORITY ORDER)\n\n1. **P0 - CRITICAL**: Fix Bug #1 - Add `composition_id` to `order_items` schema and webhook (prevents design data loss)\n2. **P0 - CRITICAL**: Fix Bug #2 - Implement client-side production export in DesignStudio (prevents low-res prints)\n3. **P1 - HIGH**: Fix Bug #3 - Add validation + error handling for design export (prevents silent failures)\n4. **P1 - HIGH**: Fix Gap #4 - Check `composition_id` in Printful fulfillment logic (ensures correct product used)\n5. **P2 - MEDIUM**: Add design audit trail (trace design → order → Printful shipment)\n6. **P2 - MEDIUM**: Add composition versioning (if user modifies design after order)\n\n---\n\n**Files Affected**: 6 critical routes + 2 components + 1 schema migration needed"}],"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":683,"cache_read_input_tokens":70791,"output_tokens":7850,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":683,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CYkReEZQ39sB1eMykkopb","type":"assistant","uuid":"6c721623-5561-4bd4-91a1-cb8fb0499dbd","timestamp":"2026-03-05T18:43:51.992Z"}
